<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img alt="demon drinks candle" class="wp-image-294701" height="250" src="https://editorial.rottentomatoes.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/EVIL-DEAD-header.jpg" width="700" /></figure>



<h1 class="wp-block-heading">Fresh Movies of 2026, Ranked by Tomatometer</h1>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">If you&#8217;ve ever wondered the difference between a film that&#8217;s &#8220;Fresh&#8221; and &#8220;Certified Fresh&#8221;, here&#8217;s the TLDR: If at least 60 percent of a film&#8217;s reviews are positive, it becomes <strong>Fresh</strong>. And <a href="https://www.rottentomatoes.com/about">when a film hits certain requirements</a>, it becomes <strong>Certified Fresh</strong>, which is our way of saying it has some of the best reviews of its era.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">But just because a movie doesn&#8217;t meet the threshold of becoming <a href="https://editorial.rottentomatoes.com/guide/best-new-movies/">Certified Fresh</a> doesn&#8217;t mean it isn&#8217;t still great. Films like <em><a href="https://www.rottentomatoes.com/m/friday">Friday</a></em>, <em><a href="https://www.rottentomatoes.com/m/castle_in_the_sky">Castle in the Sky</a></em>, and <em><a href="https://www.rottentomatoes.com/m/fifth_element">The Fifth Element</a></em> are all beloved classics — and &#8220;only&#8221; Fresh.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">2026 has had some fun Fresh films. Don’t believe me? Check out our list below of Fresh films from 2026 (that have at least 20 reviews and was released this year), where you’ll find epic <a href="https://editorial.rottentomatoes.com/guide/best-space-movies/">space adventures</a> (<a href="https://www.rottentomatoes.com/m/star_wars_the_mandalorian_and_grogu"><em>Star Wars: The Mandalorian and Grogu</em></a>), <a href="https://editorial.rottentomatoes.com/guide/best-new-horror-movies-2026/">buckets of blood</a> (<a href="https://www.rottentomatoes.com/m/they_will_kill_you"><em>They Will Kill You</em></a>), and spicy romances (<a href="https://www.rottentomatoes.com/m/you_me_and_tuscany"><em>You, Me, &amp; Tuscany</em></a>), all of which were a blast in the theater.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">We’re adding the latest Fresh films as they’re released, so check back every week, and see if you can find your favorites among them! — <a href="https://editorial.rottentomatoes.com/author/bryce-marrero/">Bryce Marrero</a></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>Recommended: <a href="https://editorial.rottentomatoes.com/guide/best-new-movies/">Best New Movies of 2026, Ranked by Tomatometer</a></strong></p>
